<div class="testimonial" data-original-title="" title="">
     At this point, <strong>I like the fact my son is allowed to have privacy</strong>. I don’t want to be a helicopter parent, but the internet is uncharted territory for this generation of parents. I want to give him space to grow, but also know if there is an issue. So far, the alerts I have received have spurred some <strong>really great conversations on topics like cyberbullying and depression</strong> in teens. He’s fine, but some of his peers are struggling. This helps me guide him in making good choices.

<h3>How It Works</h3>

        &lt;div class="row aos-init" data-aos="zoom-out" ,="" data-aos-once="true" data-aos-duration="700" data-original-title="" title=""&gt;
          <div class="col-md-10 col-md-offset-1" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="row mb-60" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="col-sm-2 number" data-original-title="" title="">
<img src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/how/step1-efc2f2b1f1cecf8dcf360b1070a8eb942f59e60deed38cd6e653dbbdc2ea8e96.png"/>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-2" data-original-title="" title="">
<img src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/how/monitor-35624dbc0fbaddc043095a52598c4cccbc139b1539ae6aa829482022659a4c54.png"/>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-8 step-container" data-original-title="" title="">
<h4 class="test">Bark automatically monitors your child</h4>
<p>
                  Connect to 24 platforms to monitor text messages, emails, and social activity for signs of harmful interactions and content.
                  <a data-original-title="" href="https://help.bark.us/general/what-bark-monitors-on-the-different-platforms" target="_blank" title="">Click here for an overview of what Bark monitors</a>.
                </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="row mb-60" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="col-sm-2 number" data-original-title="" title="">
<img src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/how/step2-b217e05898c2118e87340a2f4fba099329bf47259668ec7ac7731465c71bc70e.png"/>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-2" data-original-title="" title="">
<img src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/how/detect-98af7bd58c57010d620c162a99f6aa9c7c9f4476649ee52b4df7552fa1efc86e.png"/>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-8 step-container" data-original-title="" title="">
<h4 class="test">Bark alerts you to potential risk</h4>
<p>
                  Get automatic alerts via email and text when Bark’s algorithms detect potential risks, so you don’t have to comb through every post and text.
                </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="row mb-60" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="col-sm-2 number" data-original-title="" title="">
<img src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/how/step3-b5d51b459bd7db371c322507218c682866fe0dc91d7f42286223f2068ebe94c6.png"/>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-2" data-original-title="" title="">
<img src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/how/alert-5d0174886e48f32691605b52174db807b108108d2dc9f221bbb88512bea721a4.png"/>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-8 step-container" data-original-title="" title="">
<h4 class="test">Bark helps you solve or course-correct</h4>
<p>
                  Our expert recommendations make it easy for parents to talk to kids about digital dangers and other sensitive online issues.
                </p>
</div>
</div>

<div class="landing2-video landing2-section background-white" data-original-title="" id="video" title="">
<div class="container" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="row" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="col-sm-12 col-md-8 col-md-offset-2" data-original-title="" title="">
<h3 class="test mb-30">Reading every text message, post, &amp; email <strong>just isn't realistic</strong></h3>
<p class="mb-30 text-left">
          Technology continues to change how (and where) we communicate. Even tech-savvy parents sometimes <strong>just don’t know what apps or networks their kids are using</strong> — or who they’re talking to.
        </p>
<p class="mb-30 text-left">
          We’re parents, too. We created Bark for ourselves, and for other parents. Now it’s easier to know what your child is doing online, and keep them safe from harm.
        </p>
<iframe allowfullscreen="" class="hidden-sm hidden-xs" frameborder="0" height="365" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/5R02p9XNHm8?rel=0" width="650"></iframe>
<div class="video-container visible-xs-block visible-sm-block mb-40" data-original-title="" title="">
<iframe allowfullscreen="" autostart="" frameborder="0" height="315" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/5R02p9XNHm8?rel=0" width="560"></iframe>
</div>
<h3 class="test mt-30 mb-40"><strong>Highly Recommended by</strong> Schools, Churches, Child Psychologists &amp; Law Enforcement Professionals</h3>
<p class="mb-30 text-left">Bark’s research-backed, kid-friendly solution has won awards from The National Parenting Center, Mom’s Choice Awards, and National Parenting Product Awards.</p>
<p class="mb-30 text-left">We created Bark in close collaboration with child psychologists, youth advisors, digital media experts, and law enforcement professionals.</p>
<p class="mb-30 text-left">The result is an <strong>easy-to-use, affordable solution that doesn’t feel invasive to kids</strong>, and warns you in real-time of potential threats to your child’s health, safety, and happiness.</p>

<div class="reasons" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="row" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="col-sm-6 col-md-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<h5>UNLIMITED CHILDREN</h5>
<p>
                Whether you have one child or five, use Bark to conveniently monitor everyone’s activity in one place.
                <em>Why is is that tweens and teens have a habit of never being where they say they are?</em>
</p>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-6 col-md-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<h5>ADD UNLIMITED APPS</h5>
<p>
                Connect Bark with as many of your kids’ devices, apps, and accounts as you need. <em>Sometimes it seems like there’s a new social network every day!</em>
</p>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-6 col-md-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<h5>MONITOR ALL ACTIVITY</h5>
<p>
                Bark monitors activity on every account you connect. <em>Kids spend more and more time online as they get older.</em>
</p>
</div>
<div class="clearfix visible-sm visible-md visible-lg" data-original-title="" title=""></div>
<div class="col-sm-6 col-md-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<h5>GET ALERTS 24/7</h5>
<p>
                Bark scans your child’s accounts 24/7 and delivers alerts via text and email when it detects possible issues.
              </p>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-6 col-md-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<h5>WORLD-CLASS DATA SECURITY</h5>
<p>
                Your family’s security is one of our highest priorities. That’s why all data is kept secure with state-of-the-art SSL encryption.
              </p>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-6 col-md-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<h5>ENJOY HELPFUL, PROMPT SUPPORT</h5>
<p>
                Have a question? Need help getting set up? Our experienced support team is always here to help parents and kids get the most out of Bark.
              </p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="landing2-casestudy landing2-section" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="container" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="casestudy-intro text-center col-sm-10 col-sm-offset-1" data-original-title="" title="">
<h2 class="test mb-40">
        The Research Proves It —
        <strong>
          Families Need Bark
        </strong>
</h2>
<p>
        In 2018, <strong>we analyzed over 900 million messages</strong> across texts, email, social media, and school-issued Google and Microsoft accounts of over 2.6 million children, ages 8-17.
        These findings exclude school-based accounts. <strong>Here’s what we discovered:</strong>
</p>
</div>
<div class="row" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="text-center" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="col-sm-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="stat" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="icon-container" data-original-title="" title="">
<img id="svg" src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/casestudy/gun-fb8cac95b0e0ff393ba189143a591efe987b9ce75baec6b74fd788e24d751b8c.svg"/>
</div>
<h3>
              VIOLENCE
            </h3>
<p>
<b><span data-original-title="" title="">56.6%</span> of tweens and <span data-original-title="" title="">61.6%</span> of teens</b> expressed or encountered violent subject matter/thoughts
            </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="stat" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="icon-container" data-original-title="" title="">
<img id="svg" src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/casestudy/issues-53816ebafa5fb5ad76f3b564d9a6e3d0e97d4de8ce81b0751b4b5a598a9198e0.svg"/>
</div>
<h3>
              MENTAL HEALTH
            </h3>
<p>
<b><span data-original-title="" title="">31.9% </span>of tweens and <span data-original-title="" title="">45.6%</span> of teens</b> engaged in conversations about depression and/or anxiety
            </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="stat" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="icon-container" data-original-title="" title="">
<img id="svg" src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/casestudy/cyberbullying-34b8cd5d9103e1780e3557ae8c94540280cf06be29f95a95cfdb3601027e3967.svg"/>
</div>
<h3>
              CYBERBULLYING
            </h3>
<p>
<b><span data-original-title="" title="">62.2%</span> of tweens and <span data-original-title="" title="">70.5%</span> of teens</b> experienced cyberbullying (as a bully, victim, or witness)
            </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="stat" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="icon-container" data-original-title="" title="">
<img id="svg" src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/casestudy/drugs-809337118370530351320d5ca5b157aa93194e4076369972463794fbab193507.svg"/>
</div>
<h3>
              DRUGS/ALCOHOL
            </h3>
<p>
<b><span data-original-title="" title="">59.6%</span> of tweens and <span data-original-title="" title="">75.8%</span> of teens</b> engaged in conversations surrounding illegal drugs/alcohol
            </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="stat" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="icon-container" data-original-title="" title="">
<img id="svg" src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/casestudy/selfharm-f7856909cb278b35dcfaef0a3377209c56c8cfd897d4daa103535c3d06f1ca82.svg"/>
</div>
<h3>
              SELF-HARM/SUICIDE
            </h3>
<p>
<b><span data-original-title="" title="">23.1%</span> of tweens and <span data-original-title="" title="">35.9%</span> of teens</b> were involved with a self-harm/suicidal situation
            </p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="col-sm-4" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="stat" data-original-title="" title="">
<div class="icon-container" data-original-title="" title="">
<img id="svg" src="https://d12jofbmgge65s.cloudfront.net/assets/landing2/casestudy/sexting-09137c79736dc0740a0e6c00de40ec2ed9ef4a05a35e5b11a1651a7c1d3f00d0.svg"/>
</div>
<h3>
              SEXUAL CONTENT/SEXTING
            </h3>
<p>
<b><span data-original-title="" title="">55.9%</span> of tweens and <span data-original-title="" title="">72.1%</span> of teens</b> encountered nudity or content of a sexual nature
            </p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="testimonial" data-original-title="" title="">
     Bark [is] the <strong>best $9 I spend</strong> every month. My daughter was cyberbullied a few months ago and it’s easier to monitor any concerns without totally invading her privacy. I only get notifications if there are items of concern (sex, depression, bullying, profanity, etc.) Totally worth it!
  </div>
